- **st4ck-browse**: Drive a real browser one IPC primitive at a time via the `st4ck browse` CLI. Each subcommand is one Bash invocation; the wrapper hides the runner + FIFO behind the scenes. - **start-building**: Starts the orchestrator ([`agents/orchestrator/AGENT.md`](../agents/orchestrator/AGENT.md)). It reads the release plan in `RELEASES.yaml`, dispatches the next item's work, merges and verifies each writer's output one at a time, commits, records the evidence, and picks again. It stops at the release: it prints the merge, push, and tag commands for you and runs none of them. ([PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills](https://github.com/PranavNagrecha/AwesomeSalesforceSkills/blob/HEAD/commands/start-building.md) · ★26)
